- **pei_duo_fei**: - **persona_id**: `pei_duo_fei` - **姓名**: 裴多菲·山陀尔（Sándor Petőfi） - **原文名**: Petőfi Sándor（匈牙利语顺序） - **生卒**: 1823年1月1日 – 1849年7月31日，享年26岁 - **出生地**: 匈牙利基斯凯雷什（Kiskőrös），奥地利帝国 - **逝世地**: 特兰西瓦尼亚塞盖什瓦尔（Segesvár），奥地利帝国（今罗马尼亚锡吉什瓦拉） - **死因**: 塞盖什瓦尔战役中失踪，据信战死，遗体从未被发现 - **语言**: 匈牙利语 ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/pei_duo_fei) · ★4)
- **pei_gen**: **原文引用：** - "human knowledge and human power come to the same thing" ——《新工具》Novum Organum I:3 - "the Soueraignetie of man lyeth hidd in knowledge, wherein many things are reserued which kings with their treasures cannot buy nor with theire forces Commande" ——《论学术的推进》The Advancement of Learning - "I have taken all knowledge to be my province" ——1591年致伯利勋爵信 ([111pointer111/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/111pointer111/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/historical-persona-distiller/output/pei_gen) · ★4)

- **peirce-perspective**: 以查尔斯·桑德斯·皮尔士（Peirce）的已定位思想材料分析概念的可设想后果、信念固定与可纠错调查。 当用户明确要求该思想视角，或问题确实需要上述区分时使用；默认提供中性思想分析，人物表达须显式开启；不冒充本人。 默认中性思想；仅用户明确开启人物表达模式时加载独立表达附录，文游始终只取思想。 ([jiushiaaa/Philosophy-Skills](https://github.com/jiushiaaa/Philosophy-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/peirce-perspective))
- **peirce1-curation**: Hard-won pitfalls from building peirce1 (Essays in Pragmaticism) out of digitized journal transcriptions — transcription arbitration, sentence-splitter traps, venue pagination, per-essay bibliography, figure quoting, and the frontend shape heuristics per-node sources can trip. Use when working on peirce1 (new papers, emendations, splitter tweaks), when building any edition from digitized/transcribed text (Wikisource, Gutenberg, etc.), or when adding per-node sub-work sources to a corpus. ([scholia-study/scholia](https://github.com/scholia-study/scholia/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/peirce1-curation) · ★2)
- **peitho**: The go-to-market god. Use PEITHO to position, price, and sell anything you have built: the wedge, the offer, the landing page, the launch post, the ads, the pitch, the cold outreach. It refuses to write a headline before the positioning exists, rejects any line a competitor could have written unchanged, and makes every angle carry the cheapest test that would settle it. Trigger on "position this", "write the landing page", "how do I launch this", "price this", "/peitho". Not for deciding WHETHER to enter a market (that is ATHENA), and it never grades its own pitch (that is ZOILUS). ([eragonlonelyboy-lab/peitho](https://github.com/eragonlonelyboy-lab/peitho/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **peitho**: Use when writing or revising any prose longer than a few sentences, in any form and any medium: article, blog post, marketing copy, landing page, README, email, chapter, story, social post, release notes, or the prose parts of a spec. Also use when a draft reads flat, generic, or AI-written, when an opening has to hook a reader who can leave instantly, when persuasion has to stay honest, or when asked to audit prose for slop. Also use when writing fiction, when a long piece needs a coherence pass, or when an ending has to close rather than trail off. Carries the em-dash law, the contraction d… ([Pr1m4lc0d3/peitho](https://github.com/Pr1m4lc0d3/peitho/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **peiyin**: 给视频配音：把口播稿、旁白、解说词、台词念成音频。默认用本机的 VoxCPM2（mlx-audio，Apple Silicon，不花钱，稿子不出电脑）：每段生成后过声音门禁（嗡声、底噪、削波、语速、Whisper 对稿），不过就换种子重来，整条拼好再过一次门禁；出每段音频、整条音轨、对得上的 SRT 字幕和每段时长表（给剪辑和 Remotion 对时间用），改稿后只重做改过的段。还能用一句话描述造一个声音，或者用自己的录音克隆声音（先降噪清洗，再试念过门禁才入库）。MiniMax 云端合成作为可选后端保留。用户说「配音」「旁白」「念出来」「TTS」「语音合成」「给这条视频配个音」「用我的声音念」「出一条人声轨」「配音加字幕」「造个声音」「声音克隆」「VoxCPM」「MiniMax 配音」时使用。已经有配音、要给配音配画面动效的用 video-talkcraft；只要背景音乐或音效的用 eagle-sfx。 ([MatsuriMW/aigc-skills-and-agents](https://github.com/MatsuriMW/aigc-skills-and-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/peiyin))

- **pekko-gap-analysis**: fraktor-rsの指定モジュール（actor / stream / remote / cluster / persistence / utils などの論理名）と Apache Pekkoの参照実装を比較し、 不足機能を洗い出すギャップ分析スキル。公開API・trait・オペレーター・パターンを両側から抽出し、 カテゴリ別に分類して難易度を付与する。APIレベルのギャップが少ない場合は、内部モジュール構造 （責務分割・層配置・依存方向）の差分も分析する。 トリガー：「Pekkoと比較して不足機能を洗い出して」「gap analysis」「ギャップ分析」 「references/pekkoとの差分」「不足オペレーターを調べて」「Pekko対応状況」 「modules/{name}の不足機能」といったPekko参照実装との比較リクエストで起動。 ([j5ik2o/fraktor-rs](https://github.com/j5ik2o/fraktor-rs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pekko-gap-analysis) · ★7)
